However, the absence of distinct episodes of more severe depression and the lack of reported feelings of worthlessness or guilt make it slightly more challenging to differentiate between the two. The patient's consistent description of their condition as a steady, unrelenting state of depression and fatigue, without mention of discrete episodes of worsening symptoms, leans slightly towards Persistent Depressive Disorder, but not enough to rule out Major Depressive Disorder entirely. Further questioning is needed to assess the severity of symptoms and the presence of any potential episodes of more severe depression that might have been overlooked or underreported by the patient.", "is_final": false}
